# sudoers boundary-tokenizer sweep frontier.
#
# Written by `just sweep-sudoers-update`. This file is EVIDENCE, not
# configuration: it records the divergence set that was measured, so that the
# next Adversarial Testing Loop round starts from the last one instead of
# rebuilding it. `just sweep-sudoers` exits 1 when the measured set no longer
# matches, in EITHER direction - a new divergence and a closed one both mean
# this file is now stale.
#
# Inputs are hex because they contain VT and NBSP and this file stays ASCII.
# Every input is <LHS> = NOPASSWD: ALL, so an accepted one carries a REAL passwordless
# grant and "we said nothing" is unambiguously a miss.
#
# The ISSUE column is metadata, not part of the comparison: re-routing a known
# divergence to a different issue does not read as a frontier move. UNROUTED
# means NOT YET TRIAGED. Attributing a row to an issue is a claim about WHY it
# diverges, and a shape that merely resembles one is not evidence: issue #669
# covers `x"ab"ALL`, a single space-separated word that sudo lexes as three
# tokens, so counting words would mis-route it. Route a row when it has been
# checked, and leave it UNROUTED when it has not.
#
LANE sudoers
LENGTH 5
TOKENS 8
ALPHABET 1=ax 2=! 3=, 4=dquote 5=backslash 6=SP 7=VT(0x0b) 8=NBSP(U+00A0)
ALPHABET-EQUIV pass 2 substitutes 6=TAB(0x09) 7=FF(0x0c) to test class-equivalence
IMAGE rs-oracle9
TOTAL 32768
COUNT AGREE 28558
COUNT FALSE-FATAL 6
COUNT MISSED-GRANT 0

COUNT EQ-SPLIT 0
